{"lang_code":"en","pos":"pron","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"There used to be a lot of sand on this beach, but now there isn't as much.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["As determiner, above, but with the complement omitted and stated elsewhere or understood from context."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I devote as much of my life as I can to my family.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["With the complement indicated with 'of'."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I think she's overweight, but when I said as much to her, she got very upset.","type":"example"},{"text":"I doubt you have any claim against the bank; it says as much in the terms and conditions.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["That same thing (or something amounting to it), referring to something previously mentioned."]}],"upstream_line":3691771,"upstream_offset":9401027645,"upstream_record_sha256":"9a0e4f7fd28e591c792bbe5c6d2f00efeb82e2e20733f16360464d151dd25577","word":"as much"}

{"forms":[{"form":"radars","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"They were able to find the enemy fleet using radar.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In full primary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing radio waves (usually microwaves) which are sent towards the object and which reflect off its surfaces; also, the field of study of this method."],"tags":["uncountable"]},{"glosses":["In full primary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing radio waves (usually microwaves) which are sent towards the object and which reflect off its surfaces; also, the field of study of this method.","In full secondary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing signals transmitted by the object in response to radio waves sent towards the object."],"tags":["broadly","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The Ministry of Defence funded the development of an entirely new radar and improved avionics.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In full primary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing radio waves (usually microwaves) which are sent towards the object and which reflect off its surfaces; also, the field of study of this method.","Often preceded by a descriptive word: a system using one of the above detection methods, differentiated by configuration or platform, frequency, power, and other technical attributes."],"tags":["countable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Each of these aircraft has been upgraded with a new radar and a more powerful engine.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In full primary radar: a method of detecting a distant object and determining its position, velocity, or other characteristics by analysing radio waves (usually microwaves) which are sent towards the object and which reflect off its surfaces; also, the field of study of this method.","An installation of the apparatus for operating one of the above detection systems."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["Often preceded by a descriptive word: a natural (for example, in an animal such as a bat) or human-made detection method based on the analysis of reflected signals other than radio waves, as light waves or sound waves; (countable) an instance of this."],"tags":["broadly","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"His sensitive radar for hidden alliances keeps him out of trouble.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A superior ability to detect something; an awareness, an intuition."],"tags":["countable","figuratively"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈɹeɪdɑː/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹeɪˌdɑɹ/","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":12722,"upstream_offset":80920653,"upstream_record_sha256":"9a7217fc5353c45432783432956a99a0a661c731fb8e84e1bbcf2ff95a558cea","word":"radar"}
